# Skills · S · part 213

915 entries, smith-lang-exports to smote-imbalance-handling. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **smith-lang-exports**: What a module's `export` takes and how an export is named in C: exported functions, typedefs, tags, constants, globals; the symbol game::replay::open and the C name game_replay_open; [[std::c_name("X")]], export inline, extern with and without export, a body defined in C. Load when writing `export`, [[std::c_name]], `export inline` or `extern` in a module, or on a C name clash or an `export static` error. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-exports))
- **smith-lang-for**: The range `for` of .smith: `for (T x : xs)` and `for (Usize i, T x : xs)` over a slice, a cut or a fixed array, `T *p` to write through, `auto` and concepts, and `for constexpr (auto f : fields(T))` over a compile-time list. Load when a loop visits every element of a slice or an array, or on 'a range `for` loops over a slice', 'the body of a range `for` is a block', 'the index of a range `for` is a `Usize`'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-for))
- **smith-lang-generics**: Generic functions in .smith modules: `constexpr Type T` parameters (`arena::push(&a, Point)`), `constexpr Usize i` (a number known at compile time), `auto` parameters and `typeof(x)` (`check_equal(auto a, typeof(a) b)`), instances, the errors at a call. Load when a parameter list has `constexpr Type T` or `auto x`, on `typeof(x)`, or on 'in instance ..., requested from here', 'is called without its type', 'a type parameter is compile-time', 'is a generic function: it can only be called'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-generics))
- **smith-lang-generics-auto**: How `auto` reads its type in a generic and what converts to it: the argument's exact compiler type (any expression), `cannot tell the type of`, and the conversion rule for later arguments (a value-changing conversion is an error at the call: `the argument 300 of check_equal converts to U8`), literals judged by value. Load on 'cannot tell the type of', 'converts to ..., the type of its first argument', or when an argument of a generic has the wrong type. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-generics-auto))
- **smith-lang-generics-more**: The rest of generics in .smith: `constexpr auto f` (a function passed by name, a direct call), a computed result type (`slice_of(T, countof(rest)) f(...)`), nested calls, private names in an exported generic's body, C and C++ cannot call a generic, lowering without a compiler. Load on 'is not a function: the parameter', a generic that takes a function, or a generic whose result type is computed. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-generics-more))
- **smith-lang-if-constexpr**: `if constexpr` in .smith: in a generic's body it chooses the branch for each instance by the type (typeof(value) == f32, is_integer, sizeof, is_slice, static_assert(false) in a final else); in any other function it chooses by the target. Load on `if constexpr`, `is_integer(`, `is_float(`, a branch that must not compile for some type, or 'in `if constexpr`: ... is not a constant'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-if-constexpr))
- **smith-lang-lambda-param**: `Lambda(F)`, the parameter type for code to run (`export void sort(auto xs[:], Lambda(Bool (typeof(xs[0]) *, typeof(xs[0]) *)) less)`): a lambda or a plain function as an argument, one instance each, F naming earlier parameters' types. Load when a parameter is `Lambda(...)`, when passing `[](Num *a, Num *b) -&gt; Bool {...}` to such a function, or on 'does not have the type of `Lambda(', 'takes N parameters, but its type' or 'needs a type: write `auto'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-lambda-param))
- **smith-lang-lambdas**: Lambdas in .smith that capture nothing: `[](I32 a) -&gt; I32 { return a * 2; }`, `[](I32 a) { ... }` and `[] { ... }`, an expression that is a plain function and converts to a function pointer (qsort, a callback, a table). Load when a .smith file writes `[](` or `[] {` (also in a generic's body), passes a function pointer to C, or on 'belongs to the enclosing function', 'this `return` has another type than the lambda's' or 'smith_lambda_'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-lambdas))
- **smith-lang-methods**: Method calls in the new syntax (#1428): `xs.push(5)` for `list.push(&xs, 5)`, looked up in the module that defines the type of `xs` and in the current module; `smith lint --fix` rewrites the module-qualified call. Load when writing `x.f(args)`, on 'has no method', 'does not take a ... first', 'would be ambiguous', or when `smith lint --fix` changed `list.push(&xs, 5)`. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-methods))
- **smith-lang-modules**: Modules in .smith files: `module a::b;`, `import a::b::c as x;`, `x::name`, private names, cycles, one command for a few modules. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-modules))
- **smith-lang-modules-errors**: The errors of modules and imports in .smith, each with its fix: 'import cycle', 'cannot find module', 'is private; export it', 'must be in a file whose path ends in', 'imports come before any declaration', 'found `x.h`, which is a C header', 'the header of module lists no export', 'needs a `module` line', 'cannot be imported'. Load when smith cc or smith build rejects a module or an import. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-modules-errors))
- **smith-lang-reflection**: Reflection on a struct in a generic's body: `fields(T)` and `fields(typeof(E))` (a compile-time list of the named fields), `countof(fields(T))`, `fields(T)[i].name`, `x.[fields(T)[i]]`, `x-&gt;[...]` and `typeof(T.[...])`, so one function prints, compares or visits any struct. Load when a generic loops over the members of a struct, or on 'fields(T)', 'is not a struct', 'is a union; its members share their storage', 'has a member that is an anonymous struct or union', 'needs the compiler to read the struct'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-reflection))
- **smith-lang-reflection-field**: `fields(T)[i]` as a `Field` value in .smith: `constexpr Field f = fields(T)[i];`, `f.name`, `f.type`, `is_float(f.type)`, a function over a Field, `x-&gt;[f]`, and slice and array fields (`countof(x.[f])`, `x.[f][i]`, `x.[f][a:b]`, `typeof(x.[f][0])`). Load when reflection code stores a field in a variable, passes one to a function, or reads an element of a slice or array member. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-reflection-field))
- **smith-lang-rest**: The arguments of a generic and the loop over them: `auto... rest` (`rest...`, `rest[i]`, `countof(rest)`), `for constexpr (auto x : rest)`, `for constexpr (Usize i = 0; i &lt; countof(rest); i++)`, a type-checked loop by `typeof(rest[i])`, and why `auto args = {...}` (a built list) is gone. Load when a generic loops over its arguments, or on 'is the tuple of the arguments', 'built lists are gone', 'is out of range: `rest', 'in `for constexpr`', 'for constexpr is the loop of a generic'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-rest))
- **smith-lang-rest-expand**: Pack expansion in a generic: `pattern...` writes one copy of an expression per element of `auto... rest` (`f(leaf(rest, off)...)`, `{(F64)rest...}`), an empty pack and its comma, and the errors '`...` expands nothing', 'an expansion does not nest'. Load when a generic writes `...` after an expression that names the pack. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-rest-expand))
- **smith-lang-sized-types**: The built-in sized types of .smith files: u8 u16 u32 u64, i8 i16 i32 i64, usize, isize, f16 f32 f64, and bool. Load when you see these names in .smith source, a type error that names __UINT32_TYPE__ or unsigned long long for a u64, or a variable that is named like a sized type. - **smith-lang-slice-pointers**: A pointer to a slice as C's pointer to an array: `const char (*text)[:]` (`*text` is the slice, `(*text)[i]`, `(*text)[a:b]`, `countof(*text)`, `*text = (*text)[n:]`), `typeof(const char[:]) *text`, a typedef'd `Text *text`, passing `&s`, a cursor such as `take_line(&s, &line)`. Load when a .smith file takes or returns a slice by pointer, writes `(*p)[:]`, or on 'converting a pointer to a slice of ... discards qualifiers'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-slice-pointers))
- **smith-lang-slice-qualifiers**: Qualifiers on a slice as C has them on a pointer: `const U8 xs[:]` (read-only elements), `U8 xs[const :]` (the slice itself fixed), `U8 xs[restrict :]` (no other pointer overlaps), `const U8 src[const restrict :]`, const slices in generics and in more dimensions. Load when a .smith file has `const`, `restrict` or `static` inside `[ :]`, or on 'discards qualifiers', 'read-only variable is not assignable', 'has no meaning for a slice' or 'a qualifier goes in the first bracket'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-slice-qualifiers))

- **smith-lang-tools**: How .smith files go through smith's tools: --smith-lower and --smith-header (what the lowering writes), smith fmt and lint with names as written, smith build's headers and rebuilds, build.smith, tests and proofs named as written, and the editor and debugger. - **smith-plan**: Task-planning skill. Takes a SPECIFIC TASK as input (e.g., "refactor auth module", "ship payment service", "audit security of API gateway"), classifies it into one of 14 routing-map contexts, applies the matching methodology, and renders a structured Plan using templates/smith-plan.md.
